This selectively prunes tubelets dominated by privacy cues while preserving those most critical for action recognition. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach maintains action recognition performance comparable to models trained on raw videos, while substantially reducing privacy leakage. These results indicate that attention-driven spatiotemporal pruning offers an effective and principled solution for privacy-preserving video analytics.

</details>


### [74] [From Synthetic Data to Real Restorations: Diffusion Model for Patient-specific Dental Crown Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26588)
*Dávid Pukanec,Tibor Kubík,Michal Španěl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ToothCraft是一种基于扩散模型的牙齿冠部上下文生成方法，通过在人工创建的不完整牙齿数据上训练，实现自动化牙齿冠部修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙齿修复需要根据局部解剖结构生成完整的牙冠，但缺乏针对不完整牙齿的训练数据。现有方法难以处理广泛的牙齿缺陷类型，需要开发能够适应真实临床场景的自动化解决方案。

Method: 基于条件扩散模型框架，开发了针对3D形状的牙冠生成模型。为解决训练数据不足问题，设计了数据增强流程，从完整的牙弓公开数据集（3DS, ODD）生成不完整的牙齿几何形状，创建多样化的训练样本。

Result: 模型在合成损伤测试修复中表现出色，达到81.8%的交并比（IoU）和0.00034的Chamfer距离。可直接应用于真实病例，有效填充不完整牙齿，生成的牙冠与对颌牙列交叉最小，减少了咬合干扰风险。

Conclusion: ToothCraft通过创新的数据增强策略和扩散模型架构，成功实现了基于局部解剖上下文的自动化牙冠生成，为牙齿修复提供了有效的计算解决方案，代码和模型权重已开源。

Abstract: We present ToothCraft, a diffusion-based model for the contextual generation of tooth crowns, trained on artificially created incomplete teeth. Building upon recent advancements in conditioned diffusion models for 3D shapes, we developed a model capable of an automated tooth crown completion conditioned on local anatomical context. To address the lack of training data for this task, we designed an augmentation pipeline that generates incomplete tooth geometries from a publicly available dataset of complete dental arches (3DS, ODD). By synthesising a diverse set of training examples, our approach enables robust learning across a wide spectrum of tooth defects. Experimental results demonstrate the strong capability of our model to reconstruct complete tooth crowns, achieving an intersection over union (IoU) of 81.8% and a Chamfer Distance (CD) of 0.00034 on synthetically damaged testing restorations. Our experiments demonstrate that the model can be applied directly to real-world cases, effectively filling in incomplete teeth, while generated crowns show minimal intersection with the opposing dentition, thus reducing the risk of occlusal interference. Access to the code, model weights, and dataset information will be available at: https://github.com/ikarus1211/VISAPP_ToothCraft

</details>


### [75] [Only Whats Necessary: Pareto Optimal Data Minimization for Privacy Preserving Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26354)
*Nazia Aslam,Abhisek Ray,Thomas B. Moeslund,Kamal Nasrollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Only What's Necessary隐私设计框架，通过数据最小化机制在视频异常检测中平衡隐私保护与检测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常检测系统需要大量数据，但这些数据可能包含个人身份信息(PII)，违反GDPR的数据最小化原则，需要解决隐私保护与检测性能的平衡问题

Method: 提出基于广度和深度的数据最小化机制，通过抑制PII同时保留异常检测相关线索；使用VAD模型和隐私推断模型评估不同最小化配置，采用两种排名方法和帕累托分析来表征隐私与效用的权衡

Result: 在公开数据集上的广泛实验证明了框架的有效性，从非支配前沿识别出能最小化个人数据暴露同时保持检测性能的"甜点"操作点

Conclusion: Only What's Necessary框架为视频异常检测提供了符合GDPR要求的隐私设计解决方案，通过数据最小化机制在保护隐私的同时维持检测性能

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) systems are increasingly deployed in safety critical environments and require a large amount of data for accurate detection. However, such data may contain personally identifiable information (PII), including facial cues and sensitive demographic attributes, creating compliance challenges under the EU General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR). In particular, GDPR requires that personal data be limited to what is strictly necessary for a specified processing purpose. To address this, we introduce Only What's Necessary, a privacy-by-design framework for VAD that explicitly controls the amount and type of visual information exposed to the detection pipeline. The framework combines breadth based and depth based data minimization mechanisms to suppress PII while preserving cues relevant to anomaly detection. We evaluate a range of minimization configurations by feeding the minimized videos to both a VAD model and a privacy inference model. We employ two ranking based methods, along with Pareto analysis, to characterize the resulting trade off between privacy and utility. From the non-dominated frontier, we identify sweet spot operating points that minimize personal data exposure with limited degradation in detection performance. Extensive experiments on publicly available datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [76] [From Pen to Pixel: Translating Hand-Drawn Plots into Graphical APIs via a Novel Benchmark and Efficient Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26356)
*Zhenghao Xu,Mengning Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HDpy-13手绘图表数据集和Plot-Adapter适配器方法，解决手绘图表图像API推荐问题，降低多领域多语言场景下的参数增长和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Plot2API方法主要针对标准图表图像，忽视了非专家和初学者更易获取的手绘图表图像。现有模型在手绘图表API推荐上表现不佳，且多领域多语言场景下参数增长和计算成本过高。

Method: 1) 构建HDpy-13手绘图表数据集；2) 提出Plot-Adapter适配器方法，使用轻量CNN块增强局部特征捕获能力，通过投影矩阵共享减少微调参数，实现不同语言和领域下仅训练存储适配器而非整个模型。

Result: 实验结果表明HDpy-13数据集有效提升了手绘图表API推荐性能，Plot-Adapter在保持性能的同时显著降低了参数数量和计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: HDpy-13数据集和Plot-Adapter方法共同解决了手绘图表API推荐难题，为多领域多语言场景下的Plot2API应用提供了高效解决方案，有助于非专家和初学者更便捷地创建所需图表。

Abstract: As plots play a critical role in modern data visualization and analysis, Plot2API is launched to help non-experts and beginners create their desired plots by directly recommending graphical APIs from reference plot images by neural networks. However, previous works on Plot2API have primarily focused on the recommendation for standard plot images, while overlooking the hand-drawn plot images that are more accessible to non-experts and beginners. To make matters worse, both Plot2API models trained on standard plot images and powerful multi-modal large language models struggle to effectively recommend APIs for hand-drawn plot images due to the domain gap and lack of expertise. To facilitate non-experts and beginners, we introduce a hand-drawn plot dataset named HDpy-13 to improve the performance of graphical API recommendations for hand-drawn plot images. Additionally, to alleviate the considerable strain of parameter growth and computational resource costs arising from multi-domain and multi-language challenges in Plot2API, we propose Plot-Adapter that allows for the training and storage of separate adapters rather than requiring an entire model for each language and domain. In particular, Plot-Adapter incorporates a lightweight CNN block to improve the ability to capture local features and implements projection matrix sharing to reduce the number of fine-tuning parameters further. Experimental results demonstrate both the effectiveness of HDpy-13 and the efficiency of Plot-Adapter.

</details>


### [77] [MPDiT: Multi-Patch Global-to-Local Transformer Architecture For Efficient Flow Matching and Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26357)
*Quan Dao,Dimitris Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多块变换器设计，早期块使用大块捕获全局上下文，后期块使用小块细化局部细节，减少50%计算量同时保持良好生成性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散变换器采用各向同性设计，每个块处理相同数量的分块标记，导致训练计算负担较重，需要更高效的架构设计

Method: 提出多块变换器设计，早期块使用大块（低分辨率）捕获全局上下文，后期块使用小块（高分辨率）细化局部细节；改进时间和类别嵌入设计以加速训练收敛

Result: 在ImageNet数据集上的实验表明，该设计可将计算成本降低50%（GFLOPs），同时保持良好的生成性能，改进的嵌入设计加速了训练收敛

Conclusion: 多块变换器设计通过分层处理策略有效平衡计算效率与生成质量，为扩散模型提供更高效的架构选择

Abstract: Transformer architectures, particularly Diffusion Transformers (DiTs), have become widely used in diffusion and flow-matching models due to their strong performance compared to convolutional UNets. However, the isotropic design of DiTs processes the same number of patchified tokens in every block, leading to relatively heavy computation during training process. In this work, we introduce a multi-patch transformer design in which early blocks operate on larger patches to capture coarse global context, while later blocks use smaller patches to refine local details. This hierarchical design could reduces computational cost by up to 50\% in GFLOPs while achieving good generative performance. In addition, we also propose improved designs for time and class embeddings that accelerate training convergence. Extensive experiments on the ImageNet dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our architectural choices. Code is released at \url{https://github.com/quandao10/MPDiT}

</details>


### [78] [HandVQA: Diagnosing and Improving Fine-Grained Spatial Reasoning about Hands in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26362)
*MD Khalequzzaman Chowdhury Sayem,Mubarrat Tajoar Chowdhury,Yihalem Yimolal Tiruneh,Muneeb A. Khan,Muhammad Salman Ali,Binod Bhattarai,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HandVQA是一个大规模诊断基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在精细手部解剖理解方面的能力，包含160万道选择题，基于3D手部数据集构建，揭示了当前模型在空间推理方面的系统性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助手术、芯片制造和AR/VR人机交互等高风险场景中，理解人类手部的精细关节至关重要。尽管当前视觉语言模型在通用视觉语言基准上已达到接近人类的性能，但在精细空间推理方面仍存在困难，特别是在解释复杂的手部姿势时。

Method: 基于高质量3D手部数据集（FreiHAND、InterHand2.6M、FPHA）构建HandVQA基准，包含超过160万道受控选择题，探究手部关节之间的空间关系（如角度、距离和相对位置）。评估了多个最先进的视觉语言模型（LLaVA、DeepSeek和Qwen-VL），在基础和微调设置下使用LoRA进行轻量级微调。

Result: 评估揭示了当前模型的系统性局限，包括幻觉手指部位、错误的几何解释和泛化能力差。从该基准学习的3D基础空间知识在零样本设置下可迁移，显著提高了模型在新下游任务上的准确率：手势识别（+10.33%）和手-物体交互（+2.63%）。

Conclusion: HandVQA不仅暴露了当前视觉语言模型在精细手部空间推理方面的关键差距，还提供了经过验证的改进路径。该基准学习的3D基础空间知识能够有效迁移到下游任务，为提升模型在需要精细手部理解的实际应用中的性能提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Understanding the fine-grained articulation of human hands is critical in high-stakes settings such as robot-assisted surgery, chip manufacturing, and AR/VR-based human-AI interaction. Despite achieving near-human performance on general vision-language benchmarks, current vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with fine-grained spatial reasoning, especially in interpreting complex and articulated hand poses. We introduce HandVQA, a large-scale diagnostic benchmark designed to evaluate VLMs' understanding of detailed hand anatomy through visual question answering. Built upon high-quality 3D hand datasets (FreiHAND, InterHand2.6M, FPHA), our benchmark includes over 1.6M controlled multiple-choice questions that probe spatial relationships between hand joints, such as angles, distances, and relative positions. We evaluate several state-of-the-art VLMs (LLaVA, DeepSeek and Qwen-VL) in both base and fine-tuned settings, using lightweight fine-tuning via LoRA. Our findings reveal systematic limitations in current models, including hallucinated finger parts, incorrect geometric interpretations, and poor generalization. HandVQA not only exposes these critical reasoning gaps but provides a validated path to improvement. We demonstrate that the 3D-grounded spatial knowledge learned from our benchmark transfers in a zero-shot setting, significantly improving accuracy of model on novel downstream tasks like hand gesture recognition (+10.33%) and hand-object interaction (+2.63%).

</details>


### [79] [Restore, Assess, Repeat: A Unified Framework for Iterative Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26385)
*I-Hsiang Chen,Isma Hadji,Enrique Sanchez,Adrian Bulat,Sy-Yen Kuo,Radu Timofte,Georgios Tzimiropoulos,Brais Martinez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAR提出了一种"恢复-评估-重复"的迭代框架，将图像质量评估和图像恢复集成到统一模型中，通过潜在空间操作实现退化识别、图像恢复和质量验证，显著提升了处理未知和复合退化的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像恢复方法在处理未知或复合退化时存在泛化能力有限和效率低下的问题，且图像质量评估与恢复模块通常分离，导致延迟和信息损失。

Method: 提出RAR框架，在潜在空间中联合执行退化识别、图像恢复和质量验证，实现端到端可训练，采用迭代的"恢复-评估-重复"过程动态调整恢复策略。

Result: 在单一、未知和复合退化场景下均取得一致改进，建立了新的最先进性能，同时减少了延迟和信息损失。

Conclusion: RAR框架通过将IQA和IR紧密集成到统一模型中，实现了对未知和复合退化的高效处理，为图像恢复领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Image restoration aims to recover high quality images from inputs degraded by various factors, such as adverse weather, blur, or low light. While recent studies have shown remarkable progress across individual or unified restoration tasks, they still suffer from limited generalization and inefficiency when handling unknown or composite degradations. To address these limitations, we propose RAR, a Restore, Assess and Repeat process, that integrates Image Quality Assessment (IQA) and Image Restoration (IR) into a unified framework to iteratively and efficiently achieve high quality image restoration. Specifically, we introduce a restoration process that operates entirely in the latent domain to jointly perform degradation identification, image restoration, and quality verification. The resulting model is fully trainable end to end and allows for an all-in-one assess and restore approach that dynamically adapts the restoration process. Also, the tight integration of IQA and IR into a unified model minimizes the latency and information loss that typically arises from keeping the two modules disjoint, (e.g. during image and/or text decoding). Extensive experiments show that our approach consistent improvements under single, unknown and composite degradations, thereby establishing a new state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [80] [SHANDS: A Multi-View Dataset and Benchmark for Surgical Hand-Gesture and Error Recognition Toward Medical Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26400)
*Le Ma,Thiago Freitas dos Santos,Nadia Magnenat-Thalmann,Katarzyna Wac*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHands是一个用于外科手术手势和错误识别的大规模多视角视频数据集，旨在解决外科培训中AI评估的数据瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科培训依赖专家评估，但这种方法成本高、时间有限、难以扩展，且专业知识局限于有专家的机构。基于AI的自动评估是可行替代方案，但缺乏包含真实学员错误和多视角变异性的数据集来训练鲁棒的计算机视觉方法。

Method: 开发了SHands数据集，包含52名参与者（20名专家和32名学员）执行线性切口和缝合手术的视频，使用5个RGB相机从互补视角采集。每个参与者完成每个手术的3次标准化试验。视频在帧级别标注了15个手势基元和8种学员错误类型。

Result: 创建了大规模多视角外科手术数据集，包含手势识别和错误检测的标注。定义了单视角、多视角和跨视角泛化的标准化评估协议，并在数据集上对最先进的深度学习模型进行了基准测试。

Conclusion: SHands数据集公开发布，支持开发基于临床领域知识的鲁棒且可扩展的外科培训AI系统，解决了现有数据集的局限性。

Abstract: In surgical training for medical students, proficiency development relies on expert-led skill assessment, which is costly, time-limited, difficult to scale, and its expertise remains confined to institutions with available specialists. Automated AI-based assessment offers a viable alternative, but progress is constrained by the lack of datasets containing realistic trainee errors and the multi-view variability needed to train robust computer vision approaches. To address this gap, we present Surgical-Hands (SHands), a large-scale multi-view video dataset for surgical hand-gesture and error recognition for medical training. \textsc{SHands} captures linear incision and suturing using five RGB cameras from complementary viewpoints, performed by 52 participants (20 experts and 32 trainees), each completing three standardized trials per procedure. The videos are annotated at the frame level with 15 gesture primitives and include a validated taxonomy of 8 trainee error types, enabling both gesture recognition and error detection. We further define standardized evaluation protocols for single-view, multi-view, and cross-view generalization, and benchmark state-of-the-art deep learning models on the dataset. SHands is publicly released to support the development of robust and scalable AI systems for surgical training grounded in clinically curated domain knowledge.

</details>


### [81] [Image-based Quantification of Postural Deviations on Patients with Cervical Dystonia: A Machine Learning Approach Using Synthetic Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26444)
*Roland Stenger,Sebastian Löns,Nele Brügge,Feline Hamami,Alexander Münchau,Theresa Paulus,Anne Weissbach,Tatiana Usnich,Max Borsche,Martje G. Pauly,Lara M. Lange,Markus A. Hobert,Rebecca Herzog,Ana Luísa de Almeida Marcelino,Tina Mainka,Friederike Schumann,Lukas L. Goede,Johanna Reimer,Julienne Haas,Jos Becktepe,Alexander Baumann,Robin Wolke,Chi Wang Ip,Thorsten Odorfer,Daniel Zeller,Lisa Harder-Rauschenberger,John-Ih Lee,Philipp Albrecht,Tristan Kölsche,Joachim K. Krauss,Johanna M. Nagel,Joachim Runge,Johanna Doll-Lee,Simone Zittel,Kai Grimm,Pawel Tacik,André Lee,Tobias Bäumer,Sebastian Fudickar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 开发并验证了基于图像的自动化头位评估系统，用于客观评估颈肌张力障碍的旋转和平移症状，通过合成数据训练克服临床数据稀缺问题，在多中心研究中显示出与专家评分的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颈肌张力障碍（CD）是目前最常见的肌张力障碍形式，但现有评估主要依赖主观的临床评分量表（如TWSTRS），这些量表需要专业知识、存在主观性且部分项目评分者间信度较低。缺乏客观工具来监测疾病严重程度和治疗反应是当前临床实践的主要局限。

Method: 开发了一个结合预训练头位估计算法（用于旋转症状）和深度学习模型（用于平移症状）的评估工具。针对罕见的平移症状（特别是侧向移位），使用约16,000张合成虚拟形象图像专门训练深度学习模型，克服临床训练样本稀缺问题。在多中心研究中，使用100张真实患者图像和100张标记的合成虚拟形象数据集，将系统预测评分与20名临床专家的共识评分进行比较验证。

Result: 自动化系统在旋转症状方面与专家临床评分表现出高度一致性：斜颈（r=0.91）、侧颈（r=0.81）、前/后颈（r=0.78）。对于侧向移位，工具与临床评分的相关性为中等（r=0.55），在虚拟形象的对照基准测试中表现出比人类评分者更高的准确性。系统成功泛化到真实世界患者，提供了经过验证的客观评估工具。

Conclusion: 通过利用合成训练数据弥补临床数据缺口，该模型成功泛化到真实患者，提供了一个经过验证的、客观的颈肌张力障碍姿势评估工具，能够支持标准化的临床决策制定和临床试验评估。

Abstract: Cervical dystonia (CD) is the most common form of dystonia, yet current assessment relies on subjective clinical rating scales, such as the Toronto Western Spasmodic Torticollis Rating Scale (TWSTRS), which requires expertise, is subjective and faces low inter-rater reliability some items of the score. To address the lack of established objective tools for monitoring disease severity and treatment response, this study validates an automated image-based head pose and shift estimation system for patients with CD. We developed an assessment tool that combines a pretrained head-pose estimation algorithm for rotational symptoms with a deep learning model trained exclusively on ~16,000 synthetic avatar images to evaluate rare translational symptoms, specifically lateral shift. This synthetic data approach overcomes the scarcity of clinical training examples. The system's performance was validated in a multicenter study by comparing its predicted scores against the consensus ratings of 20 clinical experts using a dataset of 100 real patient images and 100 labeled synthetic avatars. The automated system demonstrated strong agreement with expert clinical ratings for rotational symptoms, achieving high correlations for torticollis (r=0.91), laterocollis (r=0.81), and anteroretrocollis (r=0.78). For lateral shift, the tool achieved a moderate correlation (r=0.55) with clinical ratings and demonstrated higher accuracy than human raters in controlled benchmark tests on avatars. By leveraging synthetic training data to bridge the clinical data gap, this model successfully generalizes to real-world patients, providing a validated, objective tool for CD postural assessment that can enable standardized clinical decision-making and trial evaluation.

</details>


### [82] [Meta-Learned Adaptive Optimization for Robust Human Mesh Recovery with Uncertainty-Aware Parameter Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26447)
*Shaurjya Mandal,Nutan Sharma,John Galeotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于单图像人体网格恢复的元学习框架，通过训练产生优化友好的初始化参数，并在测试时使用不确定性感知的自适应更新，显著提升了性能并降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像人体网格恢复面临深度模糊性和跨域泛化能力有限的问题。现有方法结合回归和优化方法，但在测试时优化中面临初始化不佳和参数更新效率低下的挑战。

Method: 提出元学习框架，包含三个关键创新：1) 在训练中模拟测试时优化的元学习策略，学习更好的参数初始化；2) 选择性参数缓存机制，识别并冻结已收敛的关节以减少计算开销；3) 基于分布的自适应更新，从学习到的分布中采样参数变化，实现鲁棒探索并量化不确定性。同时使用随机逼近技术处理复杂损失函数中的难解梯度。

Result: 在标准基准测试中达到最先进性能：在3DPW数据集上MPJPE降低10.3，在Human3.6M数据集上降低8.0。方法显示出优越的域适应能力，在不同环境条件下性能下降最小，同时提供与实际预测误差相关的有意义的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 结合元学习和自适应优化能够实现准确的人体网格恢复，并对具有挑战性的场景具有鲁棒的泛化能力。该方法通过优化友好的初始化和不确定性感知的自适应更新，有效解决了深度模糊性和跨域泛化问题。

Abstract: Human mesh recovery from single images remains challenging due to inherent depth ambiguity and limited generalization across domains. While recent methods combine regression and optimization approaches, they struggle with poor initialization for test-time refinement and inefficient parameter updates during optimization. We propose a novel meta-learning framework that trains models to produce optimization-friendly initializations while incorporating uncertainty-aware adaptive updates during test-time refinement. Our approach introduces three key innovations: (1) a meta-learning strategy that simulates test-time optimization during training to learn better parameter initializations, (2) a selective parameter caching mechanism that identifies and freezes converged joints to reduce computational overhead, and (3) distribution-based adaptive updates that sample parameter changes from learned distributions, enabling robust exploration while quantifying uncertainty. Additionally, we employ stochastic approximation techniques to handle intractable gradients in complex loss landscapes. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing MPJPE by 10.3 on 3DPW and 8.0 on Human3.6M compared to strong baselines. Our approach shows superior domain adaptation capabilities with minimal performance degradation across different environmental conditions, while providing meaningful uncertainty estimates that correlate with actual prediction errors. Combining meta-learning and adaptive optimization enables accurate mesh recovery and robust generalization to challenging scenarios.

</details>


### [83] [HyVIC: A Metric-Driven Spatio-Spectral Hyperspectral Image Compression Architecture Based on Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26468)
*Martin Hermann Paul Fuchs,Behnood Rasti,Begüm Demir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HyVIC架构，一种针对高光谱图像压缩的变分学习方法，通过可调节的时空编码器和解码器平衡空间与光谱特征学习，在多个压缩比下实现高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的高光谱图像压缩方法通常直接采用为自然图像设计的变分压缩模型，未能充分考虑高光谱数据特有的时空冗余特性，缺乏平衡空间与光谱特征学习的显式架构设计。

Method: 提出HyVIC架构，包含四个主要组件：1)可调节时空编码器；2)时空超编码器；3)时空超解码器；4)可调节时空解码器。采用度量驱动策略系统选择模型超参数，平衡空间与光谱特征学习。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的实验表明，该模型在广泛的压缩比范围内实现了高空间和光谱重建保真度，在BD-PSNR指标上比现有最佳方法提升高达4.66dB。

Conclusion: 空间与光谱特征学习的权衡对重建保真度至关重要，HyVIC架构通过显式设计有效利用高光谱数据的独特特性，为基于学习的变分高光谱压缩提供了实用指南和未来研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid growth of hyperspectral data archives in remote sensing (RS) necessitates effective compression methods for storage and transmission. Recent advances in learning-based hyperspectral image (HSI) compression have significantly enhanced both reconstruction fidelity and compression efficiency. However, existing methods typically adapt variational image compression models designed for natural images, without adequately accounting for the distinct spatio-spectral redundancies inherent in HSIs. In particular, they lack explicit architectural designs to balance spatial and spectral feature learning, limiting their ability to effectively leverage the unique characteristics of hyperspectral data. To address this issue, we introduce spatio-spectral variational hyperspectral image compression architecture (HyVIC). The proposed model comprises four main components: 1) adjustable spatio-spectral encoder; 2) spatio-spectral hyperencoder; 3) spatio-spectral hyperdecoder; and 4) adjustable spatio-spectral decoder. We demonstrate that the trade-off between spatial and spectral feature learning is crucial for the reconstruction fidelity, and therefore present a metric-driven strategy to systematically select the hyperparameters of the proposed model. Extensive experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed model, achieving high spatial and spectral reconstruction fidelity across a wide range of compression ratios (CRs) and improving the state of the art by up to 4.66dB in terms of BD-PSNR. Based on our results, we offer insights and derive practical guidelines to guide future research directions in learning-based variational HSI compression. Our code and pre-trained model weights are publicly available at https://git.tu-berlin.de/rsim/hyvic .

</details>


### [84] [SparseCam4D: Spatio-Temporally Consistent 4D Reconstruction from Sparse Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26481)
*Weihong Pan,Xiaoyu Zhang,Zhuang Zhang,Zhichao Ye,Nan Wang,Haomin Liu,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出稀疏相机动态重建框架，通过时空畸变场统一建模生成观测中的不一致性，实现从稀疏非标定相机输入的高质量4D重建


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高质量动态场景重建依赖密集同步相机阵列（数十至数百台），成本高昂且可扩展性差，限制了实际应用

Method: 提出稀疏相机动态重建框架，核心创新是时空畸变场，统一建模生成观测在时空维度上的不一致性；构建完整流水线支持从稀疏非标定相机输入进行4D重建

Result: 在多相机动态场景基准测试中评估，实现了时空一致的高保真渲染，显著优于现有方法

Conclusion: 通过时空畸变场有效解决生成观测的不一致问题，使高质量4D重建摆脱对昂贵密集相机阵列的依赖，提升实用可扩展性

Abstract: High-quality 4D reconstruction enables photorealistic and immersive rendering of the dynamic real world. However, unlike static scenes that can be fully captured with a single camera, high-quality dynamic scenes typically require dense arrays of tens or even hundreds of synchronized cameras. Dependence on such costly lab setups severely limits practical scalability. The reliance on such costly lab setups severely limits practical scalability. To this end, we propose a sparse-camera dynamic reconstruction framework that exploits abundant yet inconsistent generative observations. Our key innovation is the Spatio-Temporal Distortion Field, which provides a unified mechanism for modeling inconsistencies in generative observations across both spatial and temporal dimensions. Building on this, we develop a complete pipeline that enables 4D reconstruction from sparse and uncalibrated camera inputs. We evaluate our method on multi-camera dynamic scene benchmarks, achieving spatio-temporally consistent high-fidelity renderings and significantly outperforming existing approaches.

</details>


### [85] [OVI-MAP:Open-Vocabulary Instance-Semantic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26541)
*Zilong Deng,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Johanna Wald,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVI-MAP：一种解耦实例重建与语义推理的增量式开放词汇3D实例语义建图系统，实现实时运行并超越现有基准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在复杂日常环境中需要增量式开放词汇3D实例语义建图，但现有方法存在封闭集假设或密集像素级语言融合的限制，导致可扩展性和时间一致性不足

Method: 将实例重建与语义推理解耦：构建类别无关的3D实例地图（从RGB-D输入增量构建），同时仅从少量自动选择的视图中使用视觉语言模型提取语义特征

Result: 系统实现实时运行，在标准基准测试中超越了最先进的开放词汇建图基线方法

Conclusion: OVI-MAP通过解耦设计和选择性语义特征提取，实现了稳定的实例跟踪和零样本语义标注，为复杂环境中的自主智能体提供了有效的增量式开放词汇3D实例语义建图解决方案

Abstract: Incremental open-vocabulary 3D instance-semantic mapping is essential for autonomous agents operating in complex everyday environments. However, it remains challenging due to the need for robust instance segmentation, real-time processing, and flexible open-set reasoning. Existing methods often rely on the closed-set assumption or dense per-pixel language fusion, which limits scalability and temporal consistency. We introduce OVI-MAP that decouples instance reconstruction from semantic inference. We propose to build a class-agnostic 3D instance map that is incrementally constructed from RGB-D input, while semantic features are extracted only from a small set of automatically selected views using vision-language models. This design enables stable instance tracking and zero-shot semantic labeling throughout online exploration. Our system operates in real time and outperforms state-of-the-art open-vocabulary mapping baselines on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [86] [AutoWeather4D: Autonomous Driving Video Weather Conversion via G-Buffer Dual-Pass Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26546)
*Tianyu Liu,Weitao Xiong,Kunming Luo,Manyuan Zhang,Peng Liu,Yuan Liu,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoWeather4D：一种前馈3D感知天气编辑框架，通过几何与光照解耦实现自动驾驶场景的物理可控天气合成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频模型需要大量数据学习罕见天气场景，而3D感知编辑方法存在每场景优化成本高、几何与光照纠缠的问题，需要更高效的天气编辑解决方案

Method: 提出G-buffer双通道编辑机制：几何通道利用显式结构基础实现表面锚定的物理交互；光照通道解析光传输，将局部光源贡献累积到全局光照中，实现动态3D局部重光照

Result: AutoWeather4D在保持与生成式基线相当的光照真实感和结构一致性的同时，实现了细粒度的参数化物理控制，可作为自动驾驶的实用数据引擎

Conclusion: 该框架通过显式解耦几何与光照，解决了现有天气编辑方法的局限性，为自动驾驶场景提供了高效、可控的天气合成方案

Abstract: Generative video models have significantly advanced the photorealistic synthesis of adverse weather for autonomous driving; however, they consistently demand massive datasets to learn rare weather scenarios. While 3D-aware editing methods alleviate these data constraints by augmenting existing video footage, they are fundamentally bottlenecked by costly per-scene optimization and suffer from inherent geometric and illumination entanglement. In this work, we introduce AutoWeather4D, a feed-forward 3D-aware weather editing framework designed to explicitly decouple geometry and illumination. At the core of our approach is a G-buffer Dual-pass Editing mechanism. The Geometry Pass leverages explicit structural foundations to enable surface-anchored physical interactions, while the Light Pass analytically resolves light transport, accumulating the contributions of local illuminants into the global illumination to enable dynamic 3D local relighting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AutoWeather4D achieves comparable photorealism and structural consistency to generative baselines while enabling fine-grained parametric physical control, serving as a practical data engine for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [87] [HolisticSemGes: Semantic Grounding of Holistic Co-Speech Gesture Generation with Contrastive Flow-Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26553)
*Lanmiao Liu,Esam Ghaleb,Aslı Özyürek,Zerrin Yumak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于对比流匹配的共语手势生成模型，通过使用不匹配的音频-文本条件作为负样本，训练速度场遵循正确运动轨迹同时排斥语义不一致轨迹，解决了现有方法生成稀疏手势和跨模态一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共语手势生成方法存在三个主要问题：1）依赖外部语义检索方法，受限于预定义语言规则，泛化能力有限；2）流匹配方法仅使用语义一致样本优化，缺乏负样本，导致学习节奏性手势而非稀疏运动（如标志性和隐喻性手势）；3）多数方法孤立建模身体部位，无法保持跨模态一致性。

Method: 提出对比流匹配模型：1）使用不匹配的音频-文本条件作为负样本，训练速度场遵循正确运动轨迹同时排斥语义不一致轨迹；2）通过余弦和对比目标将文本、音频和整体运动嵌入到复合潜在空间，确保跨模态一致性。

Result: 在BEAT2和SHOW两个数据集上的广泛实验和用户研究表明，所提方法在生成质量和语义一致性方面优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的对比流匹配方法通过引入负样本训练和跨模态一致性机制，有效解决了共语手势生成中的语义稀疏性和跨模态一致性问题，显著提升了手势生成质量。

Abstract: While the field of co-speech gesture generation has seen significant advances, producing holistic, semantically grounded gestures remains a challenge. Existing approaches rely on external semantic retrieval methods, which limit their generalisation capability due to dependency on predefined linguistic rules. Flow-matching-based methods produce promising results; however, the network is optimised using only semantically congruent samples without exposure to negative examples, leading to learning rhythmic gestures rather than sparse motion, such as iconic and metaphoric gestures. Furthermore, by modelling body parts in isolation, the majority of methods fail to maintain crossmodal consistency. We introduce a Contrastive Flow Matching-based co-speech gesture generation model that uses mismatched audio-text conditions as negatives, training the velocity field to follow the correct motion trajectory while repelling semantically incongruent trajectories. Our model ensures cross-modal coherence by embedding text, audio, and holistic motion into a composite latent space via cosine and contrastive objectives. Extensive experiments and a user study demonstrate that our proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods on two datasets, BEAT2 and SHOW.

</details>


### [88] [Scene Grounding In the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26584)
*Tamir Cohen,Leo Segre,Shay Shomer-Chai,Shai Avidan,Hadar Averbuch-Elor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个通过语义特征将部分3D重建与完整参考模型对齐的框架，解决无重叠视图下的大规模场景重建问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从无重叠或重叠很少的野外图像重建大规模真实场景时，现有方法产生多个不连接的部分重建或错误合并非重叠区域的问题

Method: 使用Google Earth Studio的伪合成渲染作为完整参考模型，基于3D高斯泼溅表示并增强语义特征，通过基于特征的逆优化方案估计全局6DoF位姿和尺度

Result: 提出的方法在各种经典和基于学习的管道初始化下都能一致改善全局对齐，同时减轻最先进端到端模型的失败模式

Conclusion: 通过语义特征将部分重建与完整参考模型对齐的框架有效解决了无重叠视图下的大规模场景重建问题，并发布了WikiEarth数据集

Abstract: Reconstructing accurate 3D models of large-scale real-world scenes from unstructured, in-the-wild imagery remains a core challenge in computer vision, especially when the input views have little or no overlap. In such cases, existing reconstruction pipelines often produce multiple disconnected partial reconstructions or erroneously merge non-overlapping regions into overlapping geometry. In this work, we propose a framework that grounds each partial reconstruction to a complete reference model of the scene, enabling globally consistent alignment even in the absence of visual overlap. We obtain reference models from dense, geospatially accurate pseudo-synthetic renderings derived from Google Earth Studio. These renderings provide full scene coverage but differ substantially in appearance from real-world photographs. Our key insight is that, despite this significant domain gap, both domains share the same underlying scene semantics. We represent the reference model using 3D Gaussian Splatting, augmenting each Gaussian with semantic features, and formulate alignment as an inverse feature-based optimization scheme that estimates a global 6DoF pose and scale while keeping the reference model fixed. Furthermore, we introduce the WikiEarth dataset, which registers existing partial 3D reconstructions with pseudo-synthetic reference models. We demonstrate that our approach consistently improves global alignment when initialized with various classical and learning-based pipelines, while mitigating failure modes of state-of-the-art end-to-end models. All code and data will be released.

</details>


### [89] [MA-Bench: Towards Fine-grained Micro-Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26586)
*Kun Li,Jihao Gu,Fei Wang,Zhiliang Wu,Hehe Fan,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MA-Bench是首个专门用于评估多模态大语言模型在微动作理解能力的基准测试，包含1000个视频和12000个结构化问答对，采用三层评估架构，并构建了包含20.5K视频的训练数据集MA-Bench-Train用于模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大语言模型的快速发展，其在微动作理解这一人类情感分析关键领域的潜力尚未被探索，主要原因是缺乏专门的评估基准。微动作理解对于深入分析人类行为和情感至关重要。

Method: 1. 构建MA-Bench基准：包含1000个视频和12000个结构化问答对，采用三层评估架构（微动作感知、关系理解、解释推理）；2. 评估23个代表性MLLMs；3. 构建MA-Bench-Train训练数据集：包含20.5K视频和结构化微动作标注；4. 在Qwen3-VL-8B模型上进行微调实验。

Result: 1. 23个MLLMs在MA-Bench上的评估结果显示，现有模型在捕捉运动粒度和细粒度身体部位动态方面存在显著挑战；2. 在MA-Bench-Train上微调的Qwen3-VL-8B模型在微动作推理和解释任务上表现出明显的性能提升。

Conclusion: MA-Bench为推进MLLMs在理解细微微动作和人类相关行为方面建立了基础基准，填补了该领域评估工具的空白，并为通过专门训练数据提升模型性能提供了有效途径。

Abstract: With the rapid development of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), their potential in Micro-Action understanding, a vital role in human emotion analysis, remains unexplored due to the absence of specialized benchmarks. To tackle this issue, we present MA-Bench, a benchmark comprising 1,000 videos and a three-tier evaluation architecture that progressively examines micro-action perception, relational comprehension, and interpretive reasoning. MA-Bench contains 12,000 structured question-answer pairs, enabling systematic assessment of both recognition accuracy and action interpretation. The results of 23 representative MLLMs reveal that there are significant challenges in capturing motion granularity and fine-grained body-part dynamics. To address these challenges, we further construct MA-Bench-Train, a large-scale training corpus with 20.5K videos annotated with structured micro-action captions for fine-tuning MLLMs. The results of Qwen3-VL-8B fine-tuned on MA-Bench-Train show clear performance improvements across micro-action reasoning and explanation tasks. Our work aims to establish a foundation benchmark for advancing MLLMs in understanding subtle micro-action and human-related behaviors. Project Page: https://MA-Bench.github.io

</details>


### [90] [From Static to Dynamic: Exploring Self-supervised Image-to-Video Representation Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26597)
*Yang Liu,Qianqian Xu,Peisong Wen,Siran Dai,Xilin Zhao,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Co-Settle框架通过轻量级投影层在冻结的图像预训练编码器上调整表示空间，平衡视频内时间一致性和视频间语义可分性，仅需5个epoch的自监督训练即可在多个视频任务上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频表示学习方法在将图像预训练模型迁移到视频任务时面临两难：微调重模块会损害视频间语义可分性（区分不同视频中对象的能力），而减少可调参数又会阻碍视频内时间一致性（同一视频中对象的稳定表示）。这表明图像到视频迁移中存在时间一致性与语义可分性的权衡问题。

Method: 提出一致性-可分性权衡迁移学习（Co-Settle）框架，在冻结的图像预训练编码器顶部应用轻量级投影层，通过时间循环一致性目标和语义可分性约束来调整表示空间。该方法仅需少量参数调整，并提供了理论支持证明优化后的投影能在适当条件下实现更好的权衡。

Result: 在8个图像预训练模型上的实验表明，仅通过5个epoch的自监督训练，就能在多个层次的视频任务上实现一致的性能提升。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Co-Settle框架有效解决了图像到视频迁移中的时间一致性与语义可分性权衡问题，通过轻量级投影层和精心设计的优化目标，在保持模型轻量化的同时显著提升了视频表示学习性能。

Abstract: Recent studies have made notable progress in video representation learning by transferring image-pretrained models to video tasks, typically with complex temporal modules and video fine-tuning. However, fine-tuning heavy modules may compromise inter-video semantic separability, i.e., the essential ability to distinguish objects across videos. While reducing the tunable parameters hinders their intra-video temporal consistency, which is required for stable representations of the same object within a video. This dilemma indicates a potential trade-off between the intra-video temporal consistency and inter-video semantic separability during image-to-video transfer. To this end, we propose the Consistency-Separability Trade-off Transfer Learning (Co-Settle) framework, which applies a lightweight projection layer on top of the frozen image-pretrained encoder to adjust representation space with a temporal cycle consistency objective and a semantic separability constraint. We further provide a theoretical support showing that the optimized projection yields a better trade-off between the two properties under appropriate conditions. Experiments on eight image-pretrained models demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple levels of video tasks with only five epochs of self-supervised training. The code is available at https://github.com/yafeng19/Co-Settle.

</details>


### [91] [VGGRPO: Towards World-Consistent Video Generation with 4D Latent Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26599)
*Zhaochong An,Orest Kupyn,Théo Uscidda,Andrea Colaco,Karan Ahuja,Serge Belongie,Mar Gonzalez-Franco,Marta Tintore Gazulla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGRPO提出了一种潜在几何引导的视频扩散模型后训练框架，通过Latent Geometry Model连接视频扩散潜在空间与几何基础模型，在潜在空间进行几何一致性优化，避免了昂贵的VAE解码，适用于动态场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频扩散模型虽然视觉质量出色，但往往无法保持几何一致性。现有方法要么需要修改架构（影响预训练模型的泛化能力），要么仅限于静态场景且依赖RGB空间奖励（计算开销大）。需要一种既能保持预训练能力又能提升几何一致性的方法。

Method: 提出VGGRPO框架：1) Latent Geometry Model (LGM)将视频扩散潜在空间与几何基础模型连接，直接从潜在空间解码场景几何；2) 基于LGM进行潜在空间的Group Relative Policy Optimization，使用两个互补奖励：相机运动平滑性奖励（惩罚抖动轨迹）和几何重投影一致性奖励（强制跨视角几何一致性）。

Result: 在静态和动态基准测试中，VGGRPO提高了相机稳定性、几何一致性和整体质量，同时消除了昂贵的VAE解码，使潜在空间几何引导的强化学习成为高效灵活的世界一致性视频生成方法。

Conclusion: VGGRPO通过潜在几何引导的后训练框架，在保持预训练模型能力的同时显著提升了几何一致性，克服了现有方法在静态场景限制和计算开销方面的局限性，为世界一致性视频生成提供了高效灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale video diffusion models achieve impressive visual quality, yet often fail to preserve geometric consistency. Prior approaches improve consistency either by augmenting the generator with additional modules or applying geometry-aware alignment. However, architectural modifications can compromise the generalization of internet-scale pretrained models, while existing alignment methods are limited to static scenes and rely on RGB-space rewards that require repeated VAE decoding, incurring substantial compute overhead and failing to generalize to highly dynamic real-world scenes. To preserve the pretrained capacity while improving geometric consistency, we propose VGGRPO (Visual Geometry GRPO), a latent geometry-guided framework for geometry-aware video post-training. VGGRPO introduces a Latent Geometry Model (LGM) that stitches video diffusion latents to geometry foundation models, enabling direct decoding of scene geometry from the latent space. By constructing LGM from a geometry model with 4D reconstruction capability, VGGRPO naturally extends to dynamic scenes, overcoming the static-scene limitations of prior methods. Building on this, we perform latent-space Group Relative Policy Optimization with two complementary rewards: a camera motion smoothness reward that penalizes jittery trajectories, and a geometry reprojection consistency reward that enforces cross-view geometric coherence. Experiments on both static and dynamic benchmarks show that VGGRPO improves camera stability, geometry consistency, and overall quality while eliminating costly VAE decoding, making latent-space geometry-guided reinforcement an efficient and flexible approach to world-consistent video generation.

</details>


### [92] [GaussianGPT: Towards Autoregressive 3D Gaussian Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26661)
*Nicolas von Lützow,Barbara Rössle,Katharina Schmid,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussianGPT：基于Transformer的自回归模型，通过下一token预测直接生成3D高斯，实现完整3D场景生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索完全自回归的3D生成建模替代方案，以补充现有的扩散或流匹配方法，实现可控和上下文感知的3D生成

Method: 1. 使用稀疏3D卷积自编码器将高斯基元压缩为离散潜在网格并进行向量量化；2. 将token序列化，使用带3D旋转位置嵌入的因果Transformer进行建模；3. 通过下一token预测实现空间结构和外观的顺序生成

Result: 提出了一种逐步构建场景的自回归生成范式，支持补全、外延、通过温度进行可控采样以及灵活的生成范围，与神经渲染管道兼容

Conclusion: 自回归Transformer作为可控和上下文感知3D生成的补充范式，结合了自回归建模的组合归纳偏置和可扩展性，同时操作与神经渲染管道兼容的显式表示

Abstract: Most recent advances in 3D generative modeling rely on diffusion or flow-matching formulations. We instead explore a fully autoregressive alternative and introduce GaussianGPT, a transformer-based model that directly generates 3D Gaussians via next-token prediction, thus facilitating full 3D scene generation. We first compress Gaussian primitives into a discrete latent grid using a sparse 3D convolutional autoencoder with vector quantization. The resulting tokens are serialized and modeled using a causal transformer with 3D rotary positional embedding, enabling sequential generation of spatial structure and appearance. Unlike diffusion-based methods that refine scenes holistically, our formulation constructs scenes step-by-step, naturally supporting completion, outpainting, controllable sampling via temperature, and flexible generation horizons. This formulation leverages the compositional inductive biases and scalability of autoregressive modeling while operating on explicit representations compatible with modern neural rendering pipelines, positioning autoregressive transformers as a complementary paradigm for controllable and context-aware 3D generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Stabilizing Rubric Integration Training via Decoupled Advantage Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26535)
*Zelin Tan,Zhouliang Yu,Bohan Lin,Zijie Geng,Hejia Geng,Yudong Zhang,Mulei Zhang,Yang Chen,Shuyue Hu,Zhenfei Yin,Chen Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAPO是一种通过解耦优势归一化将过程级评估集成到GRPO中的方法，解决了现有奖励设计的两个限制：ORM只评估最终答案正确性而忽略推理质量，PRM直接使用会导致奖励黑客攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励设计存在两个主要问题：1）结果奖励模型（ORM）只评估最终答案正确性，将所有正确响应视为相同，忽略推理质量差异，且当所有响应都正确时优势信号逐渐消失；2）过程奖励模型（PRM）提供更丰富的监督，但直接使用PRM分数会导致奖励黑客攻击，模型通过冗长回答来提升分数而准确性崩溃。

Method: PAPO通过解耦优势归一化将过程级评估集成到GRPO中。优势由两个组件组成：1）结果组件Aout，从ORM派生并在所有响应上归一化；2）过程组件Aproc，从基于规则的PRM派生并仅在正确响应中归一化。这种解耦设计确保Aout锚定训练的正确性，而Aproc区分推理质量而不扭曲结果信号。

Result: 在多个模型规模和六个基准测试上的实验表明，PAPO始终优于ORM，在OlympiadBench上达到51.3% vs. 46.3%，且在ORM达到平台期并下降时继续改进。

Conclusion: PAPO通过解耦优势归一化有效解决了现有奖励设计的局限性，既保持了结果正确性的锚定，又实现了对推理质量的区分，在多个基准测试中表现出持续改进的优势。

Abstract: We propose Process-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO), a method that integrates process-level evaluation into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) through decoupled advantage normalization, to address two limitations of existing reward designs. Outcome reward models (ORM) evaluate only final-answer correctness, treating all correct responses identically regardless of reasoning quality, and gradually lose the advantage signal as groups become uniformly correct. Process reward models (PRM) offer richer supervision, but directly using PRM scores causes reward hacking, where models exploit verbosity to inflate scores while accuracy collapses. PAPO resolves both by composing the advantage from an outcome component Aout, derived from ORM and normalized over all responses, and a process component Aproc, derived from a rubric-based PRM and normalized exclusively among correct responses. This decoupled design ensures that Aout anchors training on correctness while Aproc differentiates reasoning quality without distorting the outcome signal. Experiments across multiple model scales and six benchmarks demonstrate that PAPO consistently outperforms ORM, reaching 51.3% vs.\ 46.3% on OlympiadBench while continuing to improve as ORM plateaus and declines.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [ETA-VLA: Efficient Token Adaptation via Temporal Fusion and Intra-LLM Sparsification for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25766)
*Yiru Wang,Anqing Jiang,Shuo Wang,Yuwen Heng,Zichong Gu,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ETA-VLA提出了一种高效的视觉语言动作模型框架，通过文本引导的稀疏聚合器动态剪枝冗余视觉token，在保持驾驶性能的同时显著降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中VLA模型需要处理历史多视角帧进行时序推理，但自注意力机制的二次计算复杂度带来了严重的计算负担，需要高效的方法来降低计算成本。

Method: 提出ETA-VLA框架，包含Intra-LLM Sparse Aggregator (ILSA)。ILSA受人类驾驶员注意力分配启发，通过文本引导评分机制和多样性保持稀疏化策略，动态识别并剪枝冗余视觉token，仅保留关键token。

Result: 在NAVSIM v2基准测试中，ETA-VLA达到与SOTA基线相当的驾驶性能，同时减少约32%的计算FLOPs。具体剪枝85%视觉token，减少61%推理FLOPs，但仍保持94%原始准确率。

Conclusion: ETA-VLA通过高效的token适应机制，在显著降低计算复杂度的同时保持了VLA模型在自动驾驶任务中的性能，为实时自动驾驶系统提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models into autonomous driving systems offers a unified framework for interpreting complex scenes and executing control commands. However, the necessity to incorporate historical multi-view frames for accurate temporal reasoning imposes a severe computational burden, primarily driven by the quadratic complexity of self-attention mechanisms in Large Language Models (LLMs). To alleviate this bottleneck, we propose ETA-VLA, an Efficient Token Adaptation framework for VLA models. ETA-VLA processes the past $n$ frames of multi-view images and introduces a novel Intra-LLM Sparse Aggregator (ILSA). Drawing inspiration from human driver attention allocation, ILSA dynamically identifies and prunes redundant visual tokens guided by textual queries and temporal consistency. Specifically, we utilize a text-guided scoring mechanism alongside a diversity-preserving sparsification strategy to select a sparse subset of critical tokens, ensuring comprehensive awareness of the driving scene. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM v2 demonstrate that ETA-VLA achieves driving performance comparable to state-of-the-art baselines while reducing computational FLOPs by approximately 32\%. Notably, our method prunes 85% of visual tokens and reduces inference FLOPs by 61\%, but still retaining 94% of the original accuracy on the NAVSIM v2 benchmark.

</details>


### [95] [Massive Parallel Deep Reinforcement Learning for Active SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25834)
*Martín Arce Llobera,Julio A. Placed,Mariano De Paula,Pablo De Cristóforis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展的端到端深度强化学习框架，用于主动SLAM，支持大规模并行训练，显著减少训练时间并支持连续动作空间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度强化学习的主动SLAM方法受限于缺乏可扩展的并行训练能力，无法充分利用GPU加速和并行计算的最新进展

Method: 开发了一个可扩展的端到端深度强化学习框架，支持大规模并行训练，能够处理连续动作空间，并探索更现实的场景

Result: 相比现有技术，该方法显著减少了训练时间，支持连续动作空间，并促进了更现实场景的探索，已作为开源框架发布

Conclusion: 提出的可扩展深度强化学习框架解决了主动SLAM中并行训练的瓶颈问题，为社区提供了可复现的开源解决方案

Abstract: Recent advances in parallel computing and GPU acceleration have created new opportunities for computation-intensive learning problems such as Active SLAM -- where actions are selected to reduce uncertainty and improve joint mapping and localization. However, existing DRL-based approaches remain constrained by the lack of scalable parallel training. In this work, we address this challenge by proposing a scalable end-to-end DRL framework for Active SLAM that enables massively parallel training. Compared with the state of the art, our method significantly reduces training time, supports continuous action spaces and facilitates the exploration of more realistic scenarios. It is released as an open-source framework to promote reproducibility and community adoption.

</details>


### [96] [Chasing Autonomy: Dynamic Retargeting and Control Guided RL for Performant and Controllable Humanoid Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25902)
*Zachary Olkin,William D. Compton,Ryan M. Bena,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过优化重定向人类运动生成周期性参考库，结合目标条件和控制引导的奖励函数，在Unitree G1机器人上实现高达3.3m/s的跑步速度和数百米户外自主导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于强化学习的仿人机器人控制器通常局限于单一运动回放，难以实现长时间自主运动。需要开发能够生成动态优化参考运动并实现稳定速度跟踪的控制方法。

Method: 提出一个流程：1) 通过带硬约束的优化程序动态重定向人类运动，从单次演示生成改进的周期性参考库；2) 研究参考运动和奖励结构对速度跟踪的影响；3) 采用目标条件和控制引导的奖励函数，跟踪动态优化的人类数据。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人硬件上部署策略，实现高达3.3m/s的跑步速度，在真实环境中穿越数百米。控制器在完整感知和规划自主堆栈中展示障碍物避让能力。

Conclusion: 动态优化的人类运动重定向结合目标条件和控制引导的奖励函数，能够实现高性能的仿人机器人跑步控制，支持高速、长距离的自主户外运动。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have the promise of locomoting like humans, including fast and dynamic running. Recently, reinforcement learning (RL) controllers that can mimic human motions have become popular as they can generate very dynamic behaviors, but they are often restricted to single motion play-back which hinders their deployment in long duration and autonomous locomotion. In this paper, we present a pipeline to dynamically retarget human motions through an optimization routine with hard constraints to generate improved periodic reference libraries from a single human demonstration. We then study the effect of both the reference motion and the reward structure on the reference and commanded velocity tracking, concluding that a goal-conditioned and control-guided reward which tracks dynamically optimized human data results in the best performance. We deploy the policy on hardware, demonstrating its speed and endurance by achieving running speeds of up to 3.3 m/s on a Unitree G1 robot and traversing hundreds of meters in real-world environments. Additionally, to demonstrate the controllability of the locomotion, we use the controller in a full perception and planning autonomy stack for obstacle avoidance while running outdoors.

</details>


### [97] [Can Vision Foundation Models Navigate? Zero-Shot Real-World Evaluation and Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25937)
*Maeva Guerrier,Karthik Soma,Jana Pavlasek,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究对五种最先进的视觉导航模型（GNM、ViNT、NoMaD、NaviBridger、CrossFormer）在真实世界环境中进行了全面评估，超越了传统的成功率指标，揭示了模型在几何理解、位置辨别和环境变化鲁棒性方面的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉导航模型的评估主要依赖成功率指标，这掩盖了轨迹质量、碰撞行为和环境变化鲁棒性等重要问题。随着模型在真实世界部署的增加，需要更全面的评估方法来揭示模型的系统性局限。

Method: 在两种机器人平台和五个室内外环境中评估五种最先进的VNM。除了成功率，还结合路径指标和基于视觉的目标识别分数，并通过控制图像扰动（运动模糊、太阳耀斑）评估鲁棒性。

Result: 发现了三个系统性局限：1）即使是架构复杂的扩散和基于Transformer的模型也频繁发生碰撞，表明几何理解有限；2）模型无法区分感知相似但语义不同的位置，导致在重复环境中出现目标预测错误；3）性能在分布偏移下显著下降。

Conclusion: 视觉导航模型在真实世界部署仍面临几何理解、位置辨别和鲁棒性等关键挑战。研究将公开评估代码库和数据集，以促进VNM的可重复基准测试。

Abstract: Visual Navigation Models (VNMs) promise generalizable, robot navigation by learning from large-scale visual demonstrations. Despite growing real-world deployment, existing evaluations rely almost exclusively on success rate, whether the robot reaches its goal, which conceals trajectory quality, collision behavior, and robustness to environmental change. We present a real-world evaluation of five state-of-the-art VNMs (GNM, ViNT, NoMaD, NaviBridger, and CrossFormer) across two robot platforms and five environments spanning indoor and outdoor settings. Beyond success rate, we combine path-based metrics with vision-based goal-recognition scores and assess robustness through controlled image perturbations (motion blur, sunflare). Our analysis uncovers three systematic limitations: (a) even architecturally sophisticated diffusion and transformer-based models exhibit frequent collisions, indicating limited geometric understanding; (b) models fail to discriminate between different locations that are perceptually similar, however some semantics differences are present, causing goal prediction errors in repetitive environments; and (c) performance degrades under distribution shift. We will publicly release our evaluation codebase and dataset to facilitate reproducible benchmarking of VNMs.

</details>


### [98] [Policy-Guided World Model Planning for Language-Conditioned Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25981)
*Amirhosein Chahe,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PiJEPA：一个两阶段框架，结合学习导航策略与潜在世界模型规划，通过策略先验初始化MPPI规划，显著提升指令条件视觉导航性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航方法存在局限性：反应式策略难以进行长时程规划，而世界模型在高维空间中动作初始化效果差。需要结合两者的优势来解决指令条件视觉导航的挑战。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 基于Octo通用策略微调，使用冻结预训练视觉编码器(DINOv2或V-JEPA-2)，在CAST导航数据集上训练，生成基于当前观测和语言指令的动作分布；2) 使用该策略分布初始化MPPI规划，在单独训练的JEPA世界模型上进行潜在状态预测规划

Result: PiJEPA显著优于独立策略执行和无先验世界模型规划，在真实世界导航任务中提高了目标到达准确性和指令跟随保真度。通过策略先验初始化MPPI，规划收敛更快，生成更高质量的动作序列。

Conclusion: PiJEMA成功结合了学习策略和世界模型规划的优势，通过策略先验初始化MPPI解决了高维空间动作初始化问题，为指令条件视觉导航提供了有效解决方案。框架对不同视觉编码器骨干(DINOv2 vs V-JEPA-2)进行了系统研究。

Abstract: Navigating to a visually specified goal given natural language instructions remains a fundamental challenge in embodied AI. Existing approaches either rely on reactive policies that struggle with long-horizon planning, or employ world models that suffer from poor action initialization in high-dimensional spaces. We present PiJEPA, a two-stage framework that combines the strengths of learned navigation policies with latent world model planning for instruction-conditioned visual navigation. In the first stage, we finetune an Octo-based generalist policy, augmented with a frozen pretrained vision encoder (DINOv2 or V-JEPA-2), on the CAST navigation dataset to produce an informed action distribution conditioned on the current observation and language instruction. In the second stage, we use this policy-derived distribution to warm-start Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) planning over a separately trained JEPA world model, which predicts future latent states in the embedding space of the same frozen encoder. By initializing the MPPI sampling distribution from the policy prior rather than from an uninformed Gaussian, our planner converges faster to high-quality action sequences that reach the goal. We systematically study the effect of the vision encoder backbone, comparing DINOv2 and V-JEPA-2, across both the policy and world model components. Experiments on real-world navigation tasks demonstrate that PiJEPA significantly outperforms both standalone policy execution and uninformed world model planning, achieving improved goal-reaching accuracy and instruction-following fidelity.

</details>


### [99] [SwarmCoDe: A Scalable Co-Design Framework for Heterogeneous Robot Swarms via Dynamic Speciation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26240)
*Andrew Wilhelm,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwarmCoDe：一种基于协同进化算法的新型机器人群体协同设计框架，通过动态物种形成和遗传标签机制，实现大规模异构机器人群体在任务规划和硬件形态上的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人群体系统具有固有的鲁棒性和执行复杂协作任务的能力，但传统框架下的大规模协同设计面临设计空间指数级增长、难以处理的挑战。需要一种可扩展的计算方法来实现大规模异构机器人群体的整体协同设计。

Method: 提出SwarmCoDe协同进化算法，采用动态物种形成机制自动扩展群体异质性以匹配任务复杂度。借鉴生物信号机制，使用进化遗传标签和选择性基因促进共生伙伴的涌现识别，无需预定义物种边界。通过进化优势基因控制群体相对组成，将物理群体规模与进化种群解耦。

Result: 成功应用于任务规划和硬件形态的同步优化，在制造预算约束下进化出最多200个智能体的专业化群体，规模达到进化种群的四倍。证明了框架在大规模异构机器人群体协同设计中的可行性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: SwarmCoDe为大规模异构机器人群体的整体协同设计提供了一条可扩展、计算可行的途径，解决了传统框架下因设计空间指数增长而难以处理的问题。

Abstract: Robot swarms offer inherent robustness and the capacity to execute complex, collaborative tasks surpassing the capabilities of single-agent systems. Co-designing these systems is critical, as marginal improvements in individual performance or unit cost compound significantly at scale. However, under traditional frameworks, this scale renders co-design intractable due to exponentially large, non-intuitive design spaces. To address this, we propose SwarmCoDe, a novel Collaborative Co-Evolutionary Algorithm (CCEA) that utilizes dynamic speciation to automatically scale swarm heterogeneity to match task complexity. Inspired by biological signaling mechanisms for inter-species cooperation, the algorithm uses evolved genetic tags and a selectivity gene to facilitate the emergent identification of symbiotically beneficial partners without predefined species boundaries. Additionally, an evolved dominance gene dictates the relative swarm composition, decoupling the physical swarm size from the evolutionary population. We apply SwarmCoDe to simultaneously optimize task planning and hardware morphology under fabrication budgets, successfully evolving specialized swarms of up to 200 agents -- four times the size of the evolutionary population. This framework provides a scalable, computationally viable pathway for the holistic co-design of large-scale, heterogeneous robot swarms.

</details>


### [100] [Line-of-Sight-Constrained Multi-Robot Mapless Navigation via Polygonal Visible Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26314)
*Ruofei Bai,Shenghai Yuan,Xinhang Xu,Xingyu Ji,Xiaowei Li,Hongliang Guo,Wei-Yun Yau,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布式多机器人导航框架，通过实时LiDAR扫描构建个体可见区域，建立视线连接约束，并引入更精确的视线距离度量和拓扑优化，在未知障碍物环境中可靠维持机器人间的视线连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人视线连接维护方法通常假设已知环境地图来制定机器人间的视线约束，这限制了其在实际部署中的应用。需要在未知障碍物环境中实现可靠的视线连接维护。

Method: 提出分布式方法：每个机器人基于实时LiDAR扫描构建自我中心的可见区域；通过分布式通信共享个体可见区域以建立机器人间的视线约束；将视线约束整合到多机器人导航框架中；引入更精确的视线距离度量；实现灵活的拓扑优化以消除冗余连接。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，该框架在充满障碍物的挑战性环境中可靠地维持了机器人间的视线连接，即使在大可见范围和脆弱最小拓扑情况下，现有方法均失败。消融研究表明拓扑优化将导航效率提升了约20%。

Conclusion: 该分布式框架能够在未知障碍物环境中有效维持多机器人系统的视线连接，通过精确的视线距离度量和拓扑优化提高了导航效率，为连接约束下的高效导航提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems rely on underlying connectivity to ensure reliable communication and timely coordination. This paper studies the line-of-sight (LoS) connectivity maintenance problem in multi-robot navigation with unknown obstacles. Prior works typically assume known environment maps to formulate LoS constraints between robots, which hinders their practical deployment. To overcome this limitation, we propose an inherently distributed approach where each robot only constructs an egocentric visible region based on its real-time LiDAR scans, instead of endeavoring to build a global map online. The individual visible regions are shared through distributed communication to establish inter-robot LoS constraints, which are then incorporated into a multi-robot navigation framework to ensure LoS-connectivity. Moreover, we enhance the robustness of connectivity maintenance by proposing a more accurate LoS-distance metric, which further enables flexible topology optimization that eliminates redundant and effort-demanding connections. The proposed framework is evaluated through extensive multi-robot navigation and exploration tasks in both simulation and real-world experiments. Results show that it reliably maintains LoS-connectivity between robots in challenging environments cluttered with obstacles, even under large visible ranges and fragile minimal topologies, where existing methods consistently fail. Ablation studies also reveal that topology optimization boosts navigation efficiency by around $20\%$, demonstrating the framework's potential for efficient navigation under connectivity constraints.

</details>


### [101] [DFM-VLA: Iterative Action Refinement for Robot Manipulation via Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26320)
*Jiayi Chen,Wenxuan Song,Shuai Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhijun Li,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DFM-VLA是一种基于离散流匹配的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过迭代精炼动作token来改进机器人操作性能，解决了现有解码范式无法修正早期token错误的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型使用离散token化方案编码动作，但无论是自回归还是离散扩散解码范式，一旦生成token就固定不变，无法在后续迭代中修正早期错误，这限制了机器人操作的性能。

Method: 提出离散流匹配VLA框架，建模token级概率速度场，动态更新完整动作序列。研究两种构建速度场的方法：辅助速度头公式和动作嵌入引导公式。采用两阶段解码策略：迭代精炼阶段后接确定性验证以确保稳定收敛。

Result: 在CALVIN、LIBERO和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，DFM-VLA在操作性能上一致优于强基线（自回归、离散扩散和连续扩散模型），同时保持高推理效率。在CALVIN上平均成功长度为4.44，在LIBERO上平均成功率为95.7%。

Conclusion: 通过离散流匹配实现动作精炼对机器人操作具有重要价值，DFM-VLA框架有效解决了现有解码范式无法修正早期token错误的问题，显著提升了操作性能。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models that encode actions using a discrete tokenization scheme are increasingly adopted for robotic manipulation, but existing decoding paradigms remain fundamentally limited. Whether actions are decoded sequentially by autoregressive VLAs or in parallel by discrete diffusion VLAs, once a token is generated, it is typically fixed and cannot be revised in subsequent iterations, so early token errors cannot be effectively corrected later. We propose DFM-VLA, a discrete flow matching VLA for iterative refinement of action tokens. DFM-VLA~models a token-level probability velocity field that dynamically updates the full action sequence across refinement iterations. We investigate two ways to construct the velocity field: an auxiliary velocity-head formulation and an action-embedding-guided formulation. Our framework further adopts a two-stage decoding strategy with an iterative refinement stage followed by deterministic validation for stable convergence. Extensive experiments on CALVIN, LIBERO, and real-world manipulation tasks show that DFM-VLA consistently outperforms strong autoregressive, discrete diffusion, and continuous diffusion baselines in manipulation performance while retaining high inference efficiency. In particular, DFM-VLA achieves an average success length of 4.44 on CALVIN and an average success rate of 95.7\% on LIBERO, highlighting the value of action refinement via discrete flow matching for robotic manipulation. Our project is available \url{https://chris1220313648.github.io/DFM-VLA/}

</details>


### [102] [DiffusionAnything: End-to-End In-context Diffusion Learning for Unified Navigation and Pre-Grasp Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26322)
*Iana Zhura,Yara Mahmoud,Jeffrin Sam,Hung Khang Nguyen,Didar Seyidov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一图像空间扩散策略，通过多尺度特征调制处理米级导航和厘米级操作，仅需每任务5分钟自监督数据，实现零样本泛化到新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉的运动规划面临挑战：传统方法需要明确目标指定和任务特定设计；最近的视觉-语言-动作模型需要大量计算资源和训练数据，且在新场景中零样本表现差。需要一种高效、轻量且能泛化的解决方案。

Method: 提出统一图像空间扩散策略，包含三个关键技术：1) 多尺度FiLM条件调节，基于任务模式、深度尺度和空间注意力实现单模型多任务行为；2) 轨迹对齐深度预测，沿生成路径点聚焦度量3D推理；3) 来自AnyTraverse的自监督注意力，无需视觉语言模型和深度传感器即可进行目标导向推理。

Result: 模型仅需RGB输入（2.0 GB内存，10 Hz），实现对新场景的鲁棒零样本泛化，适合板载部署，仅需每任务5分钟自监督数据。

Conclusion: 该方法通过统一图像空间扩散策略和多尺度特征调制，在有限数据和计算资源下实现了米级导航和厘米级操作的统一处理，为机器人运动规划提供了高效、轻量且泛化能力强的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficiently predicting motion plans directly from vision remains a fundamental challenge in robotics, where planning typically requires explicit goal specification and task-specific design. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models infer actions directly from visual input but demand massive computational resources, extensive training data, and fail zero-shot in novel scenes. We present a unified image-space diffusion policy handling both meter-scale navigation and centimeter-scale manipulation via multi-scale feature modulation, with only 5 minutes of self-supervised data per task. Three key innovations drive the framework: (1) Multi-scale FiLM conditioning on task mode, depth scale, and spatial attention enables task-appropriate behavior in a single model; (2) trajectory-aligned depth prediction focuses metric 3D reasoning along generated waypoints; (3) self-supervised attention from AnyTraverse enables goal-directed inference without vision-language models and depth sensors. Operating purely from RGB input (2.0 GB memory, 10 Hz), the model achieves robust zero-shot generalization to novel scenes while remaining suitable for onboard deployment.

</details>


### [103] [Optimal Prioritized Dissipation and Closed-Form Damping Limitation under Actuator Constraints for Haptic Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26347)
*Camilla Celli,Andrea Bini,Valerio Novelli,Alessandro Filippeschi,Francesco Porcini,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于优先级耗散动作的新阻尼限制方法，用于解决触觉交互中的稳定性与透明度权衡问题，优先考虑最小化执行器负载的最优耗散方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉交互中需要保证稳定性以确保与远程或虚拟环境的安全交互。现有的时域无源性方法（TDPA）虽然能保证稳定性，但过于保守导致透明度显著下降，且稳定化动作可能与设备的物理限制冲突。现有解决方案未能同时考虑各执行器的功率限制并最大化透明度。

Method: 提出基于优先级耗散动作的新阻尼限制方法。该方法优先考虑最小化执行器负载的最优耗散方向，任何多余的耗散被分配到正交超平面上。该方法提供封闭形式的公式，在多自由度场景中具有鲁棒性，即使在存在执行器和运动各向异性的情况下也能工作。

Result: 该方法通过使用并行触觉接口与虚拟环境交互的实验进行了验证，并在不同操作条件下进行了测试，证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于优先级耗散动作的阻尼限制方法能够有效解决触觉交互中稳定性与透明度的权衡问题，同时考虑执行器功率限制，在多自由度场景中具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: In haptics, guaranteeing stability is essential to ensure safe interaction with remote or virtual environments. One of the most relevant methods at the state-of-the-art is the Time Domain Passivity Approach (TDPA). However, its high conservatism leads to a significant degradation of transparency. Moreover, the stabilizing action may conflict with the device's physical limitations. State-of-the-art solutions have attempted to address these actuator limits, but they still fail to account simultaneously for the power limits of each actuator while maximizing transparency. This work proposes a new damping limitation method based on prioritized dissipation actions. It prioritizes an optimal dissipation direction that minimizes actuator load, while any excess dissipation is allocated to the orthogonal hyperplane. The solution provides a closed-form formulation and is robust in multi-DoF scenarios, even in the presence of actuator and motion anisotropies. The method is experimentally validated using a parallel haptic interface interacting with a virtual environment and tested under different operating conditions.

</details>


### [104] [Realtime-VLA V2: Learning to Run VLAs Fast, Smooth, and Accurate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26360)
*Chen Yang,Yucheng Hu,Yunchao Ma,Yunhuan Yang,Jing Tan,Haoqiang Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告介绍了在真实机器人上部署视觉语言动作模型（VLA）的实用技术，实现了接近人类操作速度的端到端执行性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然之前的研究解决了VLA模型在GPU上的快速神经计算问题，但如何在实际机器人上部署VLA系统仍是一个开放问题。该报告旨在提供实用的部署技术，使VLA驱动的机器人能够在需要精确性和灵巧性的真实世界任务中以接近硬件极限的速度运行

Method: 采用跨多个技术栈的实用技术：包括校准、规划与控制，以及基于学习的方法来识别最优执行速度。这些技术共同实现了端到端的快速部署

Result: 在展示的任务中，机器人执行速度达到了与人类随意操作相当的水平，并接近轻量级机械臂的硬件极限。提供了未加速的视频和推理轨迹作为验证

Conclusion: 该报告成功展示了在实际机器人上部署VLA系统的实用技术，实现了接近人类操作速度的高性能执行，为VLA模型在真实世界机器人任务中的快速部署提供了可行的解决方案

Abstract: In deployment of the VLA models to real-world robotic tasks, execution speed matters. In previous work arXiv:2510.26742 we analyze how to make neural computation of VLAs on GPU fast. However, we leave the question of how to actually deploy the VLA system on the real robots open. In this report we describe a set of practical techniques to achieve the end-to-end result of running a VLA-driven robot at an impressive speed in real world tasks that require both accuracy and dexterity. The stack of technology ranges across calibration, planning & control, and learning based method to identify optimal execution speed. In the tasks we show, the robot even executes in a speed on par with casual human operation and approaching the hardware limit of our lightweight arm. The unaccelerated videos and inference traces are provided in https://dexmal.github.io/realtime-vla-v2/.

</details>


### [105] [T-800: An 800 Hz Data Glove for Precise Hand Gesture Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26403)
*Haoyang Luo,Zihang Zhao,Leiyao Cui,Saiyao Zhang,Liu Yang,Zhi Han,Xiyuan Tang,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: T-800是一款800Hz高频数据手套系统，通过广播同步机制和应力隔离架构实现全手运动追踪，揭示了人手灵巧操作中存在>100Hz的高频运动成分，并展示了向机器人手的高保真运动映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动捕捉系统在时间分辨率和视觉遮挡之间存在权衡，无法捕捉快速、接触丰富的精细手部运动，限制了对手部灵巧操作高频动力学的研究。

Method: 开发T-800高频数据手套系统，集成基于广播的同步机制和机械应力隔离架构，在18个分布式IMU上实现800Hz同步全手运动追踪，并设计运动学重定向算法将高保真人手动作映射到灵巧机器人手模型。

Result: 系统成功恢复了先前因时间欠采样而丢失的精细操作细节，揭示人手灵巧操作中存在显著的高频运动能量（>100Hz），这些成分因先前硬件限制的奈奎斯特采样限制而无法被获取。运动学重定向算法能够准确转换高频运动数据，同时尊重机器人手的运动学约束。

Conclusion: T-800系统突破了现有运动捕捉的时空分辨率限制，为研究人手灵巧操作的高频动力学提供了新工具，其高保真运动数据可用于训练鲁棒的机器人控制策略，推动灵巧机器人操作的发展。

Abstract: Human dexterity relies on rapid, sub-second motor adjustments, yet capturing these high-frequency dynamics remains an enduring challenge in biomechanics and robotics. Existing motion capture paradigms are compromised by a trade-off between temporal resolution and visual occlusion, failing to record the fine-grained hand motion of fast, contact-rich manipulation. Here we introduce T-800, a high-bandwidth data glove system that achieves synchronized, full-hand motion tracking at 800 Hz. By integrating a novel broadcast-based synchronization mechanism with a mechanical stress isolation architecture, our system maintains sub-frame temporal alignment across 18 distributed inertial measurement units (IMUs) during extended, vigorous movements. We demonstrate that T-800 recovers fine-grained manipulation details previously lost to temporal undersampling. Our analysis reveals that human dexterity exhibits significantly high-frequency motion energy (>100 Hz) that was fundamentally inaccessible due to the Nyquist sampling limit imposed by previous hardware constraints. To validate the system's utility for robotic manipulation, we implement a kinematic retargeting algorithm that maps T-800's high-fidelity human gestures onto dexterous robotic hand models. This demonstrates that the high-frequency motion data can be accurately translated while respecting the kinematic constraints of robotic hands, providing the rich behavioral data necessary for training robust control policies in the future.

</details>


### [106] [Generalizable task-oriented object grasping through LLM-guided ontology and similarity-based planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26412)
*Hao Chen,Takuya Kiyokawa,Weiwei Wan,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何中心策略的任务导向抓取方法，通过构建对象-部件-任务本体、采样几何分析和相似性匹配框架，实现更通用的任务导向抓取，不依赖视觉语义特征，克服了视角敏感性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向抓取比简单物体抓取更具挑战性，需要精确识别物体部件并选择抓取区域以确保有效操作。现有基于视觉语言模型的方法在部件识别和抓取推理方面不稳定，限制了其在多样化物体和任务上的泛化能力。需要一种不依赖视觉语义特征、更通用的几何中心策略。

Method: 1) 使用大语言模型构建对象-部件-任务本体，基于直观人类指令进行功能部件选择；2) 基于采样的几何分析方法，从观测点云中识别选定物体部件，结合多种点分布和距离度量；3) 相似性匹配框架进行模仿抓取规划，利用具有预存分割和抓取知识的相似已知物体作为参考来指导未知目标的规划。

Result: 通过真实世界实验验证了方法在功能部件选择、识别和抓取生成方面的高准确性。展示了方法对新类别物体的泛化能力，通过扩展现有本体知识，证明了其对广泛物体和任务的适应性。

Conclusion: 提出的几何中心策略为任务导向抓取提供了更通用的解决方案，不依赖视觉语义特征，克服了模型方法的视角敏感性，通过本体构建、几何分析和相似性匹配实现了对多样化物体和任务的有效泛化。

Abstract: Task-oriented grasping (TOG) is more challenging than simple object grasping because it requires precise identification of object parts and careful selection of grasping areas to ensure effective and robust manipulation. While recent approaches have trained large-scale vision-language models to integrate part-level object segmentation with task-aware grasp planning, their instability in part recognition and grasp inference limits their ability to generalize across diverse objects and tasks. To address this issue, we introduce a novel, geometry-centric strategy for more generalizable TOG that does not rely on semantic features from visual recognition, effectively overcoming the viewpoint sensitivity of model-based approaches. Our main proposals include: 1) an object-part-task ontology for functional part selection based on intuitive human commands, constructed using a Large Language Model (LLM); 2) a sampling-based geometric analysis method for identifying the selected object part from observed point clouds, incorporating multiple point distribution and distance metrics; and 3) a similarity matching framework for imitative grasp planning, utilizing similar known objects with pre-existing segmentation and grasping knowledge as references to guide the planning for unknown targets. We validate the high accuracy of our approach in functional part selection, identification, and grasp generation through real-world experiments. Additionally, we demonstrate the method's generalization capabilities to novel-category objects by extending existing ontological knowledge, showcasing its adaptability to a broad range of objects and tasks.

</details>


### [107] [120 Minutes and a Laptop: Minimalist Image-goal Navigation via Unsupervised Exploration and Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26441)
*Xiaoming Liu,Borong Zhang,Qingbiao Li,Steven Morad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MINav：一种在消费级笔记本电脑上120分钟内完成数据收集、训练和部署的图像目标视觉导航方法，无需大规模数据集、预训练或大量计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前图像目标视觉导航范式对大规模数据集、大量预训练和计算资源的依赖，证明可以在有限资源下实现快速策略原型开发和部署。

Method: 将图像目标导航建模为离线目标条件强化学习问题，结合无监督数据收集、后见之目标重标记和离线策略学习。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，MINav提高了探索效率，在目标环境中优于零样本导航基线，且随着数据集规模增加表现更好。

Conclusion: 有效的真实世界机器人学习可以在高计算效率下实现，降低了快速策略原型开发和部署的门槛。

Abstract: The prevailing paradigm for image-goal visual navigation often assumes access to large-scale datasets, substantial pretraining, and significant computational resources. In this work, we challenge this assumption. We show that we can collect a dataset, train an in-domain policy, and deploy it to the real world (1) in less than 120 minutes, (2) on a consumer laptop, (3) without any human intervention. Our method, MINav, formulates image-goal navigation as an offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning problem, combining unsupervised data collection with hindsight goal relabeling and offline policy learning. Experiments in simulation and the real world show that MINav improves exploration efficiency, outperforms zero-shot navigation baselines in target environments, and scales favorably with dataset size. These results suggest that effective real-world robotic learning can be achieved with high computational efficiency, lowering the barrier to rapid policy prototyping and deployment.

</details>


### [108] [DTP-Attack: A decision-based black-box adversarial attack on trajectory prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26462)
*Jiaxiang Li,Jun Yan,Daniel Watzenig,Huilin Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DTP-Attack：针对轨迹预测系统的决策型黑盒对抗攻击框架，无需梯度信息，通过边界行走算法实现意图误分类和预测精度降低两种攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆安全依赖的轨迹预测系统存在对抗攻击漏洞，现有攻击方法需要白盒访问和梯度信息，且依赖刚性物理约束，限制了实际应用。需要一种更实用的黑盒攻击方法来揭示系统脆弱性

Method: 提出DTP-Attack框架，基于决策的黑盒对抗攻击方法，仅使用二进制决策输出，无需模型内部信息或梯度。采用新颖的边界行走算法在对抗区域内导航，通过邻近性保持自然维持轨迹真实性，支持意图误分类和预测精度降低两种攻击类型

Result: 在nuScenes和Apolloscape数据集上对Trajectron++和Grip++等先进模型进行评估，意图误分类攻击成功率41-81%，扰动低于0.45米；预测误差增加1.9-4.2倍。相比现有黑盒方法表现更优，在不同场景下保持高可控性和可靠性

Conclusion: DTP-Attack揭示了当前轨迹预测系统的基本脆弱性，强调了在安全关键自动驾驶应用中迫切需要鲁棒防御机制。该方法为实际场景中的对抗攻击提供了实用框架

Abstract: Trajectory prediction systems are critical for autonomous vehicle safety, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks that can cause catastrophic traffic behavior misinterpretations. Existing attack methods require white-box access with gradient information and rely on rigid physical constraints, limiting real-world applicability. We propose DTP-Attack, a decision-based black-box adversarial attack framework tailored for trajectory prediction systems. Our method operates exclusively on binary decision outputs without requiring model internals or gradients, making it practical for real-world scenarios. DTP-Attack employs a novel boundary walking algorithm that navigates adversarial regions without fixed constraints, naturally maintaining trajectory realism through proximity preservation. Unlike existing approaches, our method supports both intention misclassification attacks and prediction accuracy degradation. Extensive evaluation on nuScenes and Apolloscape datasets across state-of-the-art models including Trajectron++ and Grip++ demonstrates superior performance. DTP-Attack achieves 41 - 81% attack success rates for intention misclassification attacks that manipulate perceived driving maneuvers with perturbations below 0.45 m, and increases prediction errors by 1.9 - 4.2 for accuracy degradation. Our method consistently outperforms existing black-box approaches while maintaining high controllability and reliability across diverse scenarios. These results reveal fundamental vulnerabilities in current trajectory prediction systems, highlighting urgent needs for robust defenses in safety-critical autonomous driving applications.

</details>


### [109] [Adapt as You Say: Online Interactive Bimanual Skill Adaptation via Human Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26466)
*Zhuo Li,Dianxi Li,Tao Teng,Quentin Rouxel,Zhipeng Dong,Dennis Hong,Darwin Caldwell,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiSAIL框架通过交互式语言反馈实现离线学习的双手技能零样本在线适应，采用分层推理-调制范式，在多种双手任务和双臂平台上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能在人类生活环境中自主操作的通用机器人需要适应不断变化的任务条件，但将高维协调的双手技能适应到新的任务变体仍然是一个基本挑战。

Method: 提出BiSAIL框架，采用分层"推理-调制"范式：首先从多模态任务变体中推断广义适应目标，然后通过扩散调制调整双手运动以实现推断的目标。

Result: 在六个双手任务和两个双臂平台上的广泛真实机器人实验表明，BiSAIL在人类参与适应性、任务泛化和跨具身可扩展性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 这项工作使得非专家用户可以通过直观的语言修正灵活定制自适应双手助手，推动了适应性强、可定制的双手机器人系统的发展。

Abstract: Developing general-purpose robots capable of autonomously operating in human living environments requires the ability to adapt to continuously evolving task conditions. However, adapting high-dimensional coordinated bimanual skills to novel task variations at deployment remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we present BiSAIL (Bimanual Skill Adaptation via Interactive Language), a novel framework that enables zero-shot online adaptation of offline-learned bimanual skills through interactive language feedback. The key idea of BiSAIL is to adopt a hierarchical reason-then-modulate paradigm, which first infers generalized adaptation objectives from multimodal task variations, and then adapts bimanual motions via diffusion modulation to achieve the inferred objectives. Extensive real-robot experiments across six bimanual tasks and two dual-arm platforms demonstrate that BiSAIL significantly outperforms existing methods in human-in-the-loop adaptability, task generalization and cross-embodiment scalability. This work enables the development of adaptive bimanual assistants that can be flexibly customized by non-expert users via intuitive verbal corrections. Experimental videos and code are available at https://rip4kobe.github.io/BiSAIL/.

</details>


### [110] [Addressing Ambiguity in Imitation Learning through Product of Experts based Negative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26467)
*John Bateman,Andy M. Tyrrell,Jihong Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用次优演示和自身失败经验来学习模糊任务的负反馈模仿学习系统，相比纯正反馈系统在模拟实验中成功率提升90%，在真实机器人上提升50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中机器人任务演示通常来自非专家用户，这些演示往往是次优的。传统模仿学习假设演示来自单一高度专业专家，这在家庭机器人、辅助机器人等实际应用中不成立。需要能够利用次优演示并从自身失败中学习的系统来解决模糊任务。

Method: 提出一种负反馈模仿学习系统，能够利用次优演示并学习自身失败经验。系统通过负反馈机制改进纯正反馈模仿学习，在模糊任务中特别有效。方法在模拟和真实机器人实验中进行了验证。

Result: 在模拟实验中，相比不利用负反馈的系统，成功率提升90%；在真实机器人实验中，成功率提升50%。同时展示了比可比负反馈方案更高的效能、内存效率和时间效率。

Conclusion: 提出的负反馈模仿学习系统能够有效利用次优演示和自身失败经验来解决模糊任务，显著优于纯正反馈方法，在模拟和真实机器人实验中均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Programming robots to perform complex tasks is often difficult and time consuming, requiring expert knowledge and skills in robot software and sometimes hardware. Imitation learning is a method for training robots to perform tasks by leveraging human expertise through demonstrations. Typically, the assumption is that those demonstrations are performed by a single, highly competent expert. However, in many real-world applications that use user demonstrations for tasks or incorporate both user data and pretrained data, such as home robotics including assistive robots, this is unlikely to be the case. This paper presents research towards a system which can leverage suboptimal demonstrations to solve ambiguous tasks; and particularly learn from its own failures. This is a negative-feedback system which achieves significant improvement over purely positive imitation learning for ambiguous tasks, achieving a 90% improvement in success rate against a system that does not utilise negative feedback, compared to a 50% improvement in success rate when utilised on a real robot, as well as demonstrating higher efficacy, memory efficiency and time efficiency than a comparable negative feedback scheme. The novel scheme presented in this paper is validated through simulated and real-robot experiments.

</details>


### [111] [The Multi-AMR Buffer Storage, Retrieval, and Reshuffling Problem: Exact and Heuristic Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26542)
*Max Disselnmeyer,Thomas Bömer,Laura Dörr,Bastian Amberg,Anne Meyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高密度地面存储环境中多机器人协调的层次启发式方法，解决缓冲存储、检索和重排问题，相比精确方法大幅减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空间受限的棕地设施等密集地面存储环境中，人工操作面临劳动力严重短缺和运营成本上升的挑战。现有研究主要关注固定物品集的重排和检索，而实际制造需要能同时处理到达单元负载的自适应方法。

Method: 提出层次启发式方法：1) 使用A*搜索进行任务级序列规划，确定单元负载放置位置；2) 采用约束规划方法进行多机器人协调和调度。同时建立了二元整数规划模型用于基准测试。
